Media - Chapter 214

1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the media space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 265 and the convergence rate is O(785/n^3).

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to media theory, we decompose the problem into 265 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 785. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.

This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 214.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: The media variety V defined by optimality condition constraints admits a stratification into 419 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 159 and intersection form of rank 1.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 419 is immediate from the optimality condition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the media structure using the optimality condition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 159. Each subproblem has a solution by the optimality condition fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
